My mistake was not executing the recipe steps (pouring, stirring, adding) once I had the necessary items, and instead trying to hoard all ingredients in my inventory simultaneously, which is impossible due to the two{-}item limit. Next time, I will follow the recipe strictly: 1) Go to the storage room to get the borpulus. 2) Go to the pantry to get the apple, then immediately go to the kitchen and "put apple in cauldron". 3) Return to the pantry to get the peach, then go to the kitchen and "put peach in cauldron". 4) Go to the magic workshop to get the ice wand, then go to the kitchen and "wave ice wand on cauldron". 5) Go to the garden (or wherever the spider egg is, likely the garden or storage room based on typical logic, but I must check the garden first as I missed it) to get the spider egg, then go to the kitchen and "put spider egg in cauldron". 6) Finally, go to the garden to get the ectoplasm (or check the garden/outhouse for it), go to the kitchen, "pour ectoplasm into cauldron", and "stir with borpulus". I will process items one by one, dropping them into the cauldron immediately upon arrival in the kitchen to free up inventory space, rather than trying to carry them all at once. I must also ensure I visit the garden to find the ectoplasm and spider egg, which I completely ignored in the previous attempt.} \\
